------+    public void SpawnDrainReturnProjectile(Entity caster, Entity target, Skill skill)
------+    {
------+        if (caster == null || target == null || skill == null) return;
------+
------+        // Try to spawn return projectile effect (e.g. m_u003_c or p_u003_a)
------+        string effectName = "m_u003_c";
------+        PooledEffect drainProj = SpawnEffect(effectName);
------+        if (drainProj == null || drainProj.GameObject == null)
------+        {
------+            drainProj = SpawnEffect("p_u003_a");
------+        }
------+
------+        if (drainProj == null || drainProj.GameObject == null)
------+        {
------+            Debug.LogWarning($"[ParticleManager] Failed to create drain return projectile for skill {skill.SkillId}");
------+            return;
------+        }
------+
------+        if (drainProj == null || drainProj.GameObject == null)
------+        {
------+            Debug.LogWarning($"[ParticleManager] Failed to create drain return projectile for skill {skill.SkillId}");
------+            return;
------+        }
------+
------+        float targetCenterY = target.Appearance != null ? target.Appearance.CollisionHeight * 0.7f : 1.0f;
------+        float casterCenterY = caster.Appearance != null ? caster.Appearance.CollisionHeight * 0.7f : 1.0f;
------+
------+        Vector3 startPos = target.transform.position + Vector3.up * targetCenterY;
------+        Vector3 endPos = caster.transform.position + Vector3.up * casterCenterY;
------+
------+        drainProj.Caster = target; // Flies FROM target
------+        drainProj.Target = caster; // TO caster
------+        drainProj.IsDrainReturn = true;
------+        drainProj.IsArrow = false;
------+        drainProj.Skill = skill;
------+        drainProj.EffectClass = effectName;
------+
------+        drainProj.GameObject.transform.parent = _effectContainer.transform;
------+        drainProj.GameObject.transform.position = startPos;
------+        drainProj.GameObject.transform.localScale = Vector3.one * 0.8f;
------+        drainProj.GameObject.SetActive(true);
------+
------+        drainProj.StartingPosition = startPos;
------+        drainProj.TargetPosition = endPos;
------+        drainProj.StartTime = Time.time;
------+
------+        float distance = Vector3.Distance(startPos, endPos);
------+        float flightDuration = Mathf.Clamp(distance / 14.0f, 0.35f, 1.2f);
------+        drainProj.HitTime = Time.time + flightDuration;
------+        drainProj.EffectDurationSec = flightDuration + 0.5f;
------+        drainProj.HitSuccess = true;
------+
------+        drainProj.OnArrival = () =>
------+        {
------+            // Trigger healing burst on caster upon blood essence reaching caster
------+            PooledEffect healFx = SpawnEffect("e_u003_a");
------+            if (healFx != null && healFx.GameObject != null)
------+            {
------+                healFx.GameObject.transform.parent = _effectContainer.transform;
------+                healFx.GameObject.transform.position = caster.transform.position;
------+                healFx.GameObject.transform.localScale = Vector3.one * 1.0f;
------+                healFx.GameObject.SetActive(true);
------+                healFx.StartTime = Time.time;
------+                healFx.EffectDurationSec = 1.2f;
------+                ActiveEffects.Enqueue(healFx);
------+            }
------+            SpellDynamicLight.TriggerSelfFlash(caster, skill);
------+        };
------+
------+        drainProj.Restart();
------+        ProjectileManager.Instance.AddProjectile(drainProj);
------+        ActiveEffects.Enqueue(drainProj);
------+    }

-----+	private void showCropSalesDialog(Player player)
-----+	{
-----+		final StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
-----+		sb.append("<html><body><center><font color=\"LEVEL\">Manor Crop Exchange</font></center><br>");
-----+		
-----+		int found = 0;
-----+		for (int cropId : CastleManorManager.getInstance().getCropIds())
-----+		{
-----+			ItemInstance item = player.getInventory().getItemByItemId(cropId);
-----+			if (item != null && item.getCount() > 0)
-----+			{
-----+				found++;
-----+				Seed seed = CastleManorManager.getInstance().getSeedByCrop(cropId);
-----+				int cropPrice = (seed != null && seed.getCropReferencePrice() > 0) ? seed.getCropReferencePrice() : 100;
-----+				int r1 = (seed != null) ? seed.getReward1() : 1864; // Stem
-----+				int r2 = (seed != null) ? seed.getReward2() : 1865; // Varnish
-----+				
-----+				Item itemTmpl = ItemData.getInstance().getTemplate(cropId);
-----+				Item r1Tmpl = ItemData.getInstance().getTemplate(r1);
-----+				Item r2Tmpl = ItemData.getInstance().getTemplate(r2);
-----+				
-----+				int r1Price = (r1Tmpl != null && r1Tmpl.getReferencePrice() > 0) ? r1Tmpl.getReferencePrice() : 100;
-----+				int r2Price = (r2Tmpl != null && r2Tmpl.getReferencePrice() > 0) ? r2Tmpl.getReferencePrice() : 200;
-----+				
-----+				int r1Count = Math.max(1, (cropPrice * item.getCount()) / r1Price);
-----+				int r2Count = Math.max(1, (cropPrice * item.getCount()) / r2Price);
-----+				
-----+				String cropName = itemTmpl != null ? itemTmpl.getName() : ("Crop #" + cropId);
-----+				String r1Name = r1Tmpl != null ? r1Tmpl.getName() : ("Item #" + r1);
-----+				String r2Name = r2Tmpl != null ? r2Tmpl.getName() : ("Item #" + r2);
-----+				
-----+				sb.append("<table width=270><tr><td><b>").append(cropName).append("</b> (").append(item.getCount()).append(" pcs)</td></tr>");
-----+				sb.append("<tr><td><a action=\"bypass -h npc_").append(getObjectId()).append("_ExchangeCrop_").append(cropId).append("_1\">")
-----+				  .append("Exchange for ").append(r1Name).append(" (x").append(r1Count).append(")</a></td></tr>");
-----+				sb.append("<tr><td><a action=\"bypass -h npc_").append(getObjectId()).append("_ExchangeCrop_").append(cropId).append("_2\">")
-----+				  .append("Exchange for ").append(r2Name).append(" (x").append(r2Count).append(")</a></td></tr>");
-----+				sb.append("</table><br>");
-----+			}
-----+		}
-----+		
-----+		if (found == 0)
-----+		{
-----+			sb.append("You do not have any harvested crops in your inventory.<br><br>");
-----+			sb.append("To harvest crops:<br>");
-----+			sb.append("1. Purchase seeds from me.<br>");
-----+			sb.append("2. Purchase a Harvester.<br>");

----+	private void handlePatrol()
----+	{
----+		// Don't patrol if already moving
----+		if (_bot.isMoving())
----+		{
----+			return;
----+		}
----+
----+		// Wait 3 to 7 seconds between patrol movements
----+		final long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
----+		if (now - _lastPatrolTime < Rnd.get(3000, 7000))
----+		{
----+			return;
----+		}
----+		_lastPatrolTime = now;
----+
----+		BotSpot spot = _bot.getAssignedSpot();
----+		if (spot == null)
----+		{
----+			spot = BotSpot.KELTIR_PLAINS;
----+			_bot.setAssignedSpot(spot);
----+		}
----+
----+		final int curX = _bot.getX();
----+		final int curY = _bot.getY();
----+		final int curZ = _bot.getZ();
----+
----+		// If drifted outside spot radius, run back towards spot center
----+		if (_bot.distance2D(spot.getCenter()) > spot.getRadius())
----+		{
----+			final int tx = spot.getCenter().getX() + Rnd.get(-150, 150);
----+			final int ty = spot.getCenter().getY() + Rnd.get(-150, 150);
----+			final int tz = GeoEngine.getInstance().getHeight(tx, ty, spot.getCenter().getZ());
----+			logMoveIfNeeded("patrolling back to " + spot.getName(), tx, ty, tz);
----+			_bot.getMove().maybeMoveToLocation(new Location(tx, ty, tz), 0, true, false);
----+			return;
----+		}
----+
----+		// Pick a random walk point within 300..800 units, verified by GeoEngine
----+		for (int attempts = 0; attempts < 6; attempts++)
----+		{
----+			final double angle = Rnd.nextDouble() * 2 * Math.PI;
----+			final int dist = Rnd.get(300, 800);
----+			final int tx = (int) (curX + dist * Math.cos(angle));
----+			final int ty = (int) (curY + dist * Math.sin(angle));
----+			final int tz = GeoEngine.getInstance().getHeight(tx, ty, curZ);
----+
----+			if (spot.getCenter().distance2D(new Location(tx, ty, tz)) <= spot.getRadius()
----+				&& Math.abs(tz - curZ) < 250
----+				&& GeoEngine.getInstance().canMoveToTarget(curX, curY, curZ, tx, ty, tz))
----+			{
----+				logMoveIfNeeded("patrolling random spot", tx, ty, tz);
----+				_bot.getMove().maybeMoveToLocation(new Location(tx, ty, tz), 0, true, false);
----+				break;
----+			}
----+		}
----+	}

----+	{
----+		if (_bot.isDead() || attacker == null || attacker.isDead() || !attacker.isVisible())
----+		{
----+			return;
----+		}
----+
----+		if (_bot.isInsideZone(ZoneId.PEACE) || attacker.isInsideZone(ZoneId.PEACE))
----+		{
----+			return;
----+		}
----+
----+		if (_bot.isResting())
----+		{
----+			_bot.setResting(false);
----+			tryToStand();
----+		}
----+
----+		// Interrupt current monster farming
----+		_bot.getAttack().stop();
----+		_bot.getCast().stop();
----+
----+		// Switch target to player
----+		_bot.setTarget(attacker);
----+		_bot.setBotState(BotState.FARMING);

----+		// Retaliate
----+		performCombatAction(attacker);

----+	@Override
----+	protected void thinkAttack()
----+	{
----+		if (_bot.denyAiAction())
----+		{
----+			return;
----+		}
----+
----+		final Creature target = _currentIntention.getFinalTarget();
----+		if (isTargetLost(target))
----+		{
----+			doIdleIntention();
----+			return;
----+		}
----+
----+		if (_bot.getMove().maybeStartOffensiveFollow(target, _bot.getStatus().getPhysicalAttackRange()))
----+		{
----+			return;
----+		}
----+
----+		if ((_bot.getAttackType() == WeaponType.BOW && _bot.getAttack().isBowCoolingDown()) || _bot.getAttack().isAttackingNow())
----+		{
----+			setNextIntention(_currentIntention);
----+			return;
----+		}
----+
----+		if (!_bot.getAttack().canAttack(target))
----+		{
----+			return;
----+		}
----+
----+		_bot.getMove().stop();
----+		_bot.getAttack().doAttack(target);
----+	}

----+	@Override
----+	protected void thinkCast()

----+		}
----+
----+		if (!_bot.getCast().canCast(target, skill, _currentIntention.isCtrlPressed(), _currentIntention.getItemObjectId()))

----+			return;

----public final class RequestMakeMacro extends L2GameClientPacket
----{

----	
----	private Macro _macro;

----	
----	@Override
----	protected void readImpl()
----	{
----		int id = readD();
----		String name = readS();
----		String desc = readS();
----		String acronym = readS();
----		int icon = readC();
----		int count = readC();
----		
----		if (count > MAX_MACRO_LENGTH)
----			count = MAX_MACRO_LENGTH;
----		
----		MacroCmd[] commands = new MacroCmd[count];
----		
----		for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
----		{
----			int entry = readC();
----			int type = readC(); // 1 = skill, 3 = action, 4 = shortcut
----			int d1 = readD(); // skill or page number for shortcuts
----			int d2 = readC();
----			String command = readS();
----			
----			_commandsLenght += command.length();
----			commands[i] = new MacroCmd(entry, type, d1, d2, command);
----		}
----		_macro = new Macro(id, icon, name, desc, acronym, commands);
----	}
----	
----	@Override
----	protected void runImpl()
----	{
----		final Player player = getClient().getPlayer();
----		if (player == null)
----			return;
----		
----		// Invalid macro. Refer to the Help file for instructions.
----		if (_commandsLenght > 255)
----		{
----			player.sendPacket(SystemMessageId.INVALID_MACRO);
----			return;
----		}
----		
----		// Retrieve the Player's macros.
----		final Collection<Macro> macros = player.getMacroList().values();
----		
----		// You may create up to 24 macros.
----		if (macros.size() > 24)
----		{
----			player.sendPacket(SystemMessageId.YOU_MAY_CREATE_UP_TO_24_MACROS);
----			return;
----		}
----		
----		// Enter the name of the macro.
----		if (_macro.name.isEmpty())
----		{
----			player.sendPacket(SystemMessageId.ENTER_THE_MACRO_NAME);
----			return;
----		}
----		
----		// That name is already assigned to another macro.
----		if (macros.stream().anyMatch(m -> m.name.equalsIgnoreCase(_macro.name) && m.id != _macro.id))
----		{
----			player.sendPacket(SystemMessageId.MACRO_NAME_ALREADY_USED);
----			return;
----		}
----		
----		// Macro descriptions may contain up to 32 characters.
----		if (_macro.descr.length() > 32)
----		{
----			player.sendPacket(SystemMessageId.MACRO_DESCRIPTION_MAX_32_CHARS);
----			return;
----		}
----		
----		player.getMacroList().registerMacro(_macro);
----	}
----}
